# Change ledger: exponential-root

Every change made to `exponential-root` since the se7enxweb era began, oldest first: 1793 changes touching 10943 files. Each line gives the date, the commit, what kind of change it is, its message, and how many files it touched (+ lines added / − lines removed). Release tags are marked. The narrative documentation (features, bc notes, specifications, the history chronicle) explains what these changes deliver; this ledger is the complete record they are built from.

| Updated | 1332 |
| Added | 257 |
| Merged | 99 |
| Other | 86 |
| Removed | 14 |
| Renamed | 5 |
